<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="768" data-end="1314"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="relative h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="select-none sticky z-2 top-(--sticky-padding-top)"><div class="flex w-full items-center justify-between py-1.5 ps-4 pe-1.5 font-sans md:ps-5 bg-(--code-block-surface)"><div class="flex max-w-[75%] min-w-0 cursor-default items-center text-sm font-medium justify-self-start text-token-text-primary"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-sm me-2.5 shrink-0"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#72c6a7" fill="currentColor"></use></svg>gitignore</div><div class="flex flex-row items-center gap-0.5 justify-self-end"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div></div></div><div class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-4 top-12 bottom-4"><div class="pointer-events-none sticky z-40 shrink-0 z-1!" style="top: calc(calc(var(--sticky-padding-top) + 48px) - 1px * 3); transform: none;"><div class=""><div class="relative"><div class="" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span># ==============================
# AeroSim Git Ignore
# ==============================

# Python cache
__pycache__/
*.py[cod]
*.pyo

# Python virtual environments
venv/
.venv/
env/

# C compiled files
*.o
*.obj
*.exe
*.dll
*.so
*.dylib

# Build directories
build/
dist/

# IDE / Editor
.vscode/
.idea/

# OS generated files
.DS_Store
Thumbs.db

# Python testing/cache
.pytest_cache/

# Generated plots / temporary output
*.png
*.jpg
*.jpeg
*.tmp
*.log

# Environment variables / secrets
.env
.env.*

# Temporary files
*.bak
*.swp</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<p data-start="1316" data-end="1348">Then <strong data-start="1321" data-end="1329">Save</strong> and close Notepad.</p>

transform: none;"><div class=""><div class="relative"><div class="" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span># AeroSim ✈️

## Aircraft Performance &amp; Mission Simulator

AeroSim is a physics-based aircraft performance and mission simulation project built around a C physics engine with Python-based analysis, visualization, and interactive interfaces.

The goal is to create a modular aerospace simulation platform based on standard aircraft performance equations.

---

## 🏗️ Architecture

```text
              AeroSim
                 │
        ┌────────┴────────┐
        │                 │
      Python             C
        │                 │
 Analysis / UI      Physics Engine
 Visualization           │
        │          ┌──────┼──────┐
        │          │      │      │
        │     Atmosphere Aero  Propulsion
        │          │      │      │
        │          └──────┼──────┘
        │                 │
        │            Performance
        │                 │
        └──── ctypes ─────┤
                          │
                    Mission Engine</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>

<h2 data-start="4800" data-end="4812">🎯 Vision</h2>
<p data-start="4814" data-end="4964">The long-term goal of AeroSim is to evolve from a physics calculation engine into an interactive aircraft performance and mission simulation platform.</p>
<p data-start="4966" data-end="4999">The project focuses on combining:</p>
<p data-start="5001" data-end="5090"><strong data-start="5001" data-end="5090">Aerospace Physics + Systems Programming + Data Visualization + Interactive Simulation</strong></p>
<hr data-start="5092" data-end="5095">
<h2 data-start="5097" data-end="5113">⚠️ Disclaimer</h2>
<p data-start="5115" data-end="5221">AeroSim is an educational and engineering simulation project using simplified aircraft performance models.</p>
<p data-start="5223" data-end="5336">It is not intended for aircraft certification, operational flight planning, or real-world flight decision-making.</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="5337" data-end="5435"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>

<h2 data-start="7447" data-end="7491">🧨 One thing I want you to do differently</h2>

margin-bottom: calc(-4px); height: calc(4px); mask-image: linear-gradient(transparent 25%, white 75%);"><div class="sticky bg-token-border-light" style="top: calc(var(--sticky-padding-top) + 48px); height: 1px;"></div></div></div><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class=""><div class="relative"><div class="" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span># ✈️ AeroSim

## Aircraft Performance &amp; Mission Simulator

AeroSim is a physics-based aircraft performance and mission simulation platform built around a modular **C physics engine** with **Python-based analysis, visualization, and interactive interfaces**.

The project is being developed to explore how fundamental aerospace engineering equations can be transformed into a practical, modular simulation system.

&gt; **Aerospace Physics × Systems Programming × Simulation × Data Visualization**

---

## 🚀 Project Vision

The goal of AeroSim is to evolve from a collection of aircraft performance calculations into an interactive simulation platform capable of modelling aircraft performance across different flight conditions and mission phases.

The long-term vision includes:

- Interactive aircraft configuration
- Physics-based flight simulation
- Live telemetry
- Performance analysis
- Mission planning
- Interactive aircraft visualization
- Web-based simulation interface
- Engineering report generation

AeroSim is being developed incrementally, with the physics engine serving as the foundation for all higher-level functionality.

---

# 🧠 Architecture

AeroSim follows a layered architecture designed to keep the physics calculations independent from the user interface.

```text
                         ┌─────────────────────┐
                         │      AeroSim UI     │
                         │  Desktop / Web UI   │
                         └──────────┬──────────┘
                                    │
                                    ▼
                         ┌─────────────────────┐
                         │       Python        │
                         │ Analysis / Plotting │
                         │   UI / Orchestration│
                         └──────────┬──────────┘
                                    │
                              ctypes / API
                                    │
                                    ▼
                         ┌─────────────────────┐
                         │    C Physics Core   │
                         └──────────┬──────────┘
                                    │
              ┌─────────────────────┼─────────────────────┐
              │                     │                     │
              ▼                     ▼                     ▼
       ┌─────────────┐       ┌─────────────┐       ┌─────────────┐
       │ Atmosphere  │       │Aerodynamics │       │ Propulsion  │
       └─────────────┘       └─────────────┘       └─────────────┘
              │                     │                     │
              └─────────────────────┼─────────────────────┘
                                    ▼
                         ┌─────────────────────┐
                         │    Performance      │
                         └──────────┬──────────┘
                                    │
                                    ▼
                         ┌─────────────────────┐
                         │ Mission / Scenario  │
                         └─────────────────────┘</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<h3 data-start="3294" data-end="3314" class="" style="">Design Principle</h3>
<p data-start="3316" data-end="3366">The core aircraft physics is implemented in <strong data-start="3360" data-end="3365">C</strong>.</p>
<p data-start="3368" data-end="3394">Python is responsible for:</p>
<ul data-start="3396" data-end="3513">
<li data-start="3396" data-end="3424">
<p data-start="3398" data-end="3424">Calling the physics engine</p>
</li>
<li data-start="3425" data-end="3442">
<p data-start="3427" data-end="3442">Data processing</p>
</li>
<li data-start="3443" data-end="3453">
<p data-start="3445" data-end="3453">Analysis</p>
</li>
<li data-start="3454" data-end="3469">
<p data-start="3456" data-end="3469">Visualization</p>
</li>
<li data-start="3470" data-end="3486">
<p data-start="3472" data-end="3486">User interface</p>
</li>
<li data-start="3487" data-end="3513">
<p data-start="3489" data-end="3513">Simulation orchestration</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="3515" data-end="3604">This separation allows the physics engine to remain reusable across different interfaces.</p>
<hr data-start="3606" data-end="3609">
<h1 data-start="3611" data-end="3636">⚙️ Current Capabilities</h1>
<h2 data-start="3638" data-end="3661">🌡️ Atmosphere Model</h2>
<p data-start="3663" data-end="3770">AeroSim currently implements a simplified International Standard Atmosphere model for the lower atmosphere.</p>
<p data-start="3772" data-end="3805">The atmosphere module calculates:</p>
<ul data-start="3807" data-end="3862">
<li data-start="3807" data-end="3820">
<p data-start="3809" data-end="3820">Temperature</p>
</li>
<li data-start="3821" data-end="3831">
<p data-start="3823" data-end="3831">Pressure</p>
</li>
<li data-start="3832" data-end="3845">
<p data-start="3834" data-end="3845">Air density</p>
</li>
<li data-start="3846" data-end="3862">
<p data-start="3848" data-end="3862">Speed of sound</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="3864" data-end="3969">The model is used as the environmental foundation for subsequent aerodynamic and propulsion calculations.</p>
<hr data-start="3971" data-end="3974">
<h2 data-start="3976" data-end="3995">🛩️ Aerodynamics</h2>
<p data-start="3997" data-end="4078">The aerodynamic module implements fundamental aircraft performance relationships.</p>
<p data-start="4080" data-end="4109">Current calculations include:</p>
<ul data-start="4111" data-end="4278">
<li data-start="4111" data-end="4138">
<p data-start="4113" data-end="4138">Aircraft weight from mass</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4139" data-end="4157">
<p data-start="4141" data-end="4157">Dynamic pressure</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4158" data-end="4176">
<p data-start="4160" data-end="4176">Lift coefficient</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4177" data-end="4205">
<p data-start="4179" data-end="4205">Zero-lift drag coefficient</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4206" data-end="4220">
<p data-start="4208" data-end="4220">Induced drag</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4221" data-end="4243">
<p data-start="4223" data-end="4243">Parabolic drag polar</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4244" data-end="4250">
<p data-start="4246" data-end="4250">Lift</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4251" data-end="4257">
<p data-start="4253" data-end="4257">Drag</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4258" data-end="4278">
<p data-start="4260" data-end="4278">Lift-to-drag ratio</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="4280" data-end="4308">The current drag model uses:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="4310" data-end="4338"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>CD = CD0 + k CL²</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<p data-start="4340" data-end="4346">where:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="4348" data-end="4376"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>k = 1 / (π e AR)</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<p data-start="4378" data-end="4465">This provides a simple but useful representation of aircraft parasite and induced drag.</p>
<hr data-start="4467" data-end="4470">
<h2 data-start="4472" data-end="4488">🔥 Propulsion</h2>
<p data-start="4490" data-end="4538">AeroSim includes a generic jet propulsion model.</p>
<p data-start="4540" data-end="4571">Current functionality includes:</p>
<ul data-start="4573" data-end="4661">
<li data-start="4573" data-end="4589">
<p data-start="4575" data-end="4589">Maximum thrust</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4590" data-end="4608">
<p data-start="4592" data-end="4608">Throttle control</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4609" data-end="4615">
<p data-start="4611" data-end="4615">TSFC</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4616" data-end="4627">
<p data-start="4618" data-end="4627">Fuel flow</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4628" data-end="4646">
<p data-start="4630" data-end="4646">Altitude effects</p>
</li>
<li data-start="4647" data-end="4661">
<p data-start="4649" data-end="4661">Mach effects</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="4663" data-end="4763">The propulsion model is intentionally generic rather than representing a specific real-world engine.</p>
<hr data-start="4765" data-end="4768">
<h2 data-start="4770" data-end="4796">📈 Aircraft Performance</h2>
<p data-start="4798" data-end="4883">The performance module combines atmospheric, aerodynamic and propulsion calculations.</p>
<p data-start="4885" data-end="4914" class="" style="">Current capabilities include:</p>
<h3 data-start="4916" data-end="4933" class="" style="">Excess Thrust</h3>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="4935" data-end="4963"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>T_excess = T - D</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<h3 data-start="4965" data-end="4981">Excess Power</h3>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="4983" data-end="5014"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>P_excess = (T - D)V</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<h3 data-start="5016" data-end="5033">Rate of Climb</h3>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="5035" data-end="5065"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>ROC = (T - D)V / W</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<h3 data-start="5067" data-end="5082">Stall Speed</h3>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="5084" data-end="5118"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>Vs = √(2W / (ρSCLmax))</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<h3 data-start="5120" data-end="5150">Maximum Level-Flight Speed</h3>
<p data-start="5152" data-end="5210">The simulator numerically searches for the velocity where:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="5212" data-end="5229"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>T = D</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<h3 data-start="5231" data-end="5244">Jet Range</h3>
<p data-start="5246" data-end="5306">AeroSim includes a simplified jet Breguet range formulation.</p>
<h3 data-start="5308" data-end="5325">Jet Endurance</h3>
<p data-start="5327" data-end="5402">A corresponding constant-condition jet endurance model is also implemented.</p>
<hr data-start="5404" data-end="5407">
<h1 data-start="5409" data-end="5433">🛰️ Mission Simulation</h1>
<p data-start="5435" data-end="5492">AeroSim includes a time-stepped mission simulation layer.</p>
<p data-start="5494" data-end="5623">The mission system allows aircraft state variables to evolve over simulation time while tracking relevant performance quantities.</p>
<p data-start="5625" data-end="5656">Current functionality includes:</p>
<ul data-start="5658" data-end="5820">
<li data-start="5658" data-end="5682">
<p data-start="5660" data-end="5682">Scenario configuration</p>
</li>
<li data-start="5683" data-end="5706">
<p data-start="5685" data-end="5706">Mission configuration</p>
</li>
<li data-start="5707" data-end="5732">
<p data-start="5709" data-end="5732">Time-stepped simulation</p>
</li>
<li data-start="5733" data-end="5759">
<p data-start="5735" data-end="5759">Aircraft state evolution</p>
</li>
<li data-start="5760" data-end="5775">
<p data-start="5762" data-end="5775">Fuel tracking</p>
</li>
<li data-start="5776" data-end="5792">
<p data-start="5778" data-end="5792">Mission phases</p>
</li>
<li data-start="5793" data-end="5820">
<p data-start="5795" data-end="5820">Simulation result storage</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="5822" data-end="5930">The mission layer is designed to become the foundation for more advanced mission planning and visualization.</p>
<hr data-start="5932" data-end="5935">
<h1 data-start="5937" data-end="5964">🐍 Python ↔ C Integration</h1>
<p data-start="5966" data-end="6057">AeroSim uses Python's <code data-start="5988" data-end="5996">ctypes</code> interface to communicate with the compiled C physics engine.</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="6059" data-end="6155"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>Python
   │
   │ ctypes
   ▼
AeroSim C Shared Library
   │
   ▼
Physics Calculations</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<p data-start="6157" data-end="6278" class="" style="">This allows the Python layer to access the C engine while keeping the actual physics implementation inside the C modules.</p>
<p data-start="6280" data-end="6310">The bridge explicitly defines:</p>
<ul data-start="6312" data-end="6412">
<li data-start="6312" data-end="6326">
<p data-start="6314" data-end="6326">C structures</p>
</li>
<li data-start="6327" data-end="6352">
<p data-start="6329" data-end="6352">Function argument types</p>
</li>
<li data-start="6353" data-end="6367">
<p data-start="6355" data-end="6367">Return types</p>
</li>
<li data-start="6368" data-end="6384">
<p data-start="6370" data-end="6384">Error handling</p>
</li>
<li data-start="6385" data-end="6412">
<p data-start="6387" data-end="6412">Physics-engine interfaces</p>
</li>
</ul>
<hr data-start="6414" data-end="6417">
<h1 data-start="6419" data-end="6448">📊 Visualization &amp; Analysis</h1>
<p data-start="6450" data-end="6528">Python is used to transform simulation output into engineering visualizations.</p>
<p data-start="6530" data-end="6573">Current visualization capabilities include:</p>
<ul data-start="6575" data-end="6697">
<li data-start="6575" data-end="6603">
<p data-start="6577" data-end="6603">Aircraft performance plots</p>
</li>
<li data-start="6604" data-end="6623">
<p data-start="6606" data-end="6623">Altitude profiles</p>
</li>
<li data-start="6624" data-end="6642">
<p data-start="6626" data-end="6642">Mission profiles</p>
</li>
<li data-start="6643" data-end="6663">
<p data-start="6645" data-end="6663">Performance curves</p>
</li>
<li data-start="6664" data-end="6697">
<p data-start="6666" data-end="6697">Simulation result visualization</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="6699" data-end="6769">The plotting layer is intentionally separated from the physics engine.</p>
<p data-start="6771" data-end="6782">This means:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="6784" data-end="6837"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>Physics → Data → Analysis → Visualization</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<p data-start="6839" data-end="6908">rather than mixing visualization logic with the physics calculations.</p>
<hr data-start="6910" data-end="6913">
<h1 data-start="6915" data-end="6938">🖥️ Current Interface</h1>
<p data-start="6940" data-end="7024">AeroSim currently includes an interactive desktop interface built using <strong data-start="7012" data-end="7023">PySide6</strong>.</p>
<p data-start="7026" data-end="7049">The interface provides:</p>
<ul data-start="7051" data-end="7182">
<li data-start="7051" data-end="7075">
<p data-start="7053" data-end="7075">Aircraft configuration</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7076" data-end="7101">
<p data-start="7078" data-end="7101">Flight-condition inputs</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7102" data-end="7123">
<p data-start="7104" data-end="7123">Simulation controls</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7124" data-end="7154">
<p data-start="7126" data-end="7154">C-engine performance results</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7155" data-end="7182">
<p data-start="7157" data-end="7182">Performance visualization</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="7184" data-end="7314">The desktop interface is currently serving as the prototype environment while the next-generation web interface is being designed.</p>
<hr data-start="7316" data-end="7319">
<h1 data-start="7321" data-end="7333">🧪 Testing</h1>
<p data-start="7335" data-end="7425">AeroSim is developed using modular testing rather than relying only on end-to-end testing.</p>
<p data-start="7427" data-end="7467">Each major C module has dedicated tests.</p>
<p data-start="7469" data-end="7500">Current tested modules include:</p>
<ul data-start="7502" data-end="7577">
<li data-start="7502" data-end="7514">
<p data-start="7504" data-end="7514">Atmosphere</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7515" data-end="7529">
<p data-start="7517" data-end="7529">Aerodynamics</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7530" data-end="7542">
<p data-start="7532" data-end="7542">Propulsion</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7543" data-end="7556">
<p data-start="7545" data-end="7556">Performance</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7557" data-end="7567">
<p data-start="7559" data-end="7567">Scenario</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7568" data-end="7577">
<p data-start="7570" data-end="7577">Mission</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="7579" data-end="7598">Python tests cover:</p>
<ul data-start="7600" data-end="7677">
<li data-start="7600" data-end="7622">
<p data-start="7602" data-end="7622">C/Python integration</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7623" data-end="7647">
<p data-start="7625" data-end="7647">Analysis functionality</p>
</li>
<li data-start="7648" data-end="7677">
<p data-start="7650" data-end="7677">Visualization functionality</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="7679" data-end="7835">The project follows an incremental development approach where each physics module is validated before being integrated into higher-level simulation systems.</p>
<hr data-start="7837" data-end="7840">
<h1 data-start="7842" data-end="7865">🗂️ Project Structure</h1>
<p data-start="7867" data-end="7925">The current project is organized approximately as follows:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="7927" data-end="8537"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>AeroSim/

<hr data-start="8929" data-end="8932">
<h1 data-start="8934" data-end="8961">🧩 Development Philosophy</h1>
<p data-start="8963" data-end="9027">AeroSim is being developed using a modular engineering approach.</p>
<p data-start="9029" data-end="9126">Instead of building the entire simulator at once, the project is divided into independent layers:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="9128" data-end="9281"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>Atmosphere
     ↓
Aerodynamics
     ↓
Propulsion
     ↓
Performance
     ↓
Mission
     ↓
Analysis
     ↓
Visualization
     ↓
User Interface</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<p data-start="9283" data-end="9376" class="" style="">Each layer is developed and tested independently before being integrated with the next layer.</p>
<p data-start="9378" data-end="9410">This makes the system easier to:</p>
<ul data-start="9412" data-end="9454">
<li data-start="9412" data-end="9418">
<p data-start="9414" data-end="9418">Test</p>
</li>
<li data-start="9419" data-end="9426">
<p data-start="9421" data-end="9426">Debug</p>
</li>
<li data-start="9427" data-end="9435">
<p data-start="9429" data-end="9435">Extend</p>
</li>
<li data-start="9436" data-end="9443">
<p data-start="9438" data-end="9443">Reuse</p>
</li>
<li data-start="9444" data-end="9454">
<p data-start="9446" data-end="9454">Maintain</p>
</li>
</ul>
<hr data-start="9456" data-end="9459">
<h1 data-start="9461" data-end="9474">🗺️ Roadmap</h1>
<h2 data-start="9476" data-end="9507">Phase 1 — Physics Foundation</h2>
<ul class="contains-task-list" data-start="9509" data-end="9703">
<li class="task-list-item" data-start="9509" data-end="9529">
<p data-start="9515" data-end="9529"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> ISA atmosphere</p>
</li>
<li class="task-list-item" data-start="9530" data-end="9560">
<p data-start="9536" data-end="9560"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Aerodynamic calculations</p>

<hr data-start="10898" data-end="10901">
<h1 data-start="10903" data-end="10924">🎯 Long-Term Vision</h1>
<p data-start="10926" data-end="11110">The long-term goal of AeroSim is to create a modular aircraft simulation environment where users can move seamlessly from aircraft configuration to simulation and engineering analysis.</p>
<p data-start="11112" data-end="11137">The intended workflow is:</p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="11139" data-end="11379"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>Select Aircraft
      ↓
Configure Aircraft
      ↓
Configure Flight Condition
      ↓
Run Simulation
      ↓
Live Telemetry
      ↓
Performance Analysis
      ↓
Mission Planning
      ↓
Mission Results
      ↓
Engineering Report</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>
<p data-start="11381" data-end="11437" class="" style="">The eventual platform is envisioned as a combination of:</p>
<p data-start="11439" data-end="11531"><strong data-start="11439" data-end="11531">Aircraft Engineering + Physics Simulation + Mission Analysis + Interactive Visualization</strong></p>
<hr data-start="11533" data-end="11536">
<h1 data-start="11538" data-end="11567">⚠️ Model Scope &amp; Disclaimer</h1>
<p data-start="11569" data-end="11626">AeroSim uses simplified mathematical models intended for:</p>
<ul data-start="11628" data-end="11742">
<li data-start="11628" data-end="11639">
<p data-start="11630" data-end="11639">Education</p>
</li>
<li data-start="11640" data-end="11669">
<p data-start="11642" data-end="11669">Aerospace engineering study</p>
</li>
<li data-start="11670" data-end="11692">
<p data-start="11672" data-end="11692">Software development</p>
</li>
<li data-start="11693" data-end="11720">
<p data-start="11695" data-end="11720">Numerical experimentation</p>
</li>
<li data-start="11721" data-end="11742">
<p data-start="11723" data-end="11742">Simulation research</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="11744" data-end="11846">The current models do not represent a certified aircraft performance model and should not be used for:</p>
<ul data-start="11848" data-end="11949">
<li data-start="11848" data-end="11867">
<p data-start="11850" data-end="11867">Flight operations</p>
</li>
<li data-start="11868" data-end="11892">
<p data-start="11870" data-end="11892">Aircraft certification</p>
</li>
<li data-start="11893" data-end="11920">
<p data-start="11895" data-end="11920">Safety-critical decisions</p>
</li>
<li data-start="11921" data-end="11949">
<p data-start="11923" data-end="11949">Real-world flight planning</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p data-start="11951" data-end="12075">Real aircraft performance depends on substantially more factors than the simplified models currently represented in AeroSim.</p>
<hr data-start="12077" data-end="12080">
<h1 data-start="12082" data-end="12108">👨‍💻 Development Status</h1>
<p data-start="12110" data-end="12135"><strong data-start="12110" data-end="12135">🚧 Active Development</strong></p>
<p data-start="12137" data-end="12262">AeroSim is currently transitioning from a modular aircraft performance engine into a broader interactive simulation platform.</p>
<p data-start="12264" data-end="12388">The physics core is being developed first, followed by analysis, visualization, interface and mission-planning capabilities.</p>
<hr data-start="12390" data-end="12393">
<h1 data-start="12395" data-end="12407">📜 License</h1>
<p data-start="12409" data-end="12460">This project is currently under active development.</p>
<p data-start="12462" data-end="12544">License information will be added as the project reaches its first public release.</p>
<hr data-start="12546" data-end="12549">
<h2 data-start="12551" data-end="12564">✈️ AeroSim</h2>
<p data-start="12566" data-end="12599"><strong data-start="12566" data-end="12599">From equations to simulation.</strong></p>
<pre class="overflow-visible! px-0!" data-start="12601" data-end="12669"><div class="relative w-full mt-4 mb-1"><div class=""><div class="contents"><div class="relative"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0"><div class="h-full min-h-0 min-w-0" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"><div class="border border-token-border-light border-radius-3xl corner-superellipse/1.1 rounded-3xl"><div class="h-full w-full border-radius-3xl bg-(--code-block-surface) corner-superellipse/1.1 overflow-clip rounded-3xl [--code-block-surface:var(--bg-elevated-secondary)] dark:[--code-block-surface:var(--composer-surface-primary)] lxnfua_clipPathFallback"><div class="pointer-events-none absolute end-1.5 top-1 z-2 md:end-2 md:top-1"><button type="button" class="flex gap-1 items-center select-none pointer-events-auto py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 size-9 rounded-full px-2" aria-label="Copy" data-state="closed"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" aria-hidden="true" class="icon-md"><use href="/cdn/assets/sprites-core-e289d166.svg#7fccfb" fill="currentColor"></use></svg></button></div><div class="relative"><div class="pe-11 pt-3" style="transform: none;"><div class="relative z-0 flex max-w-full"><div id="code-block-viewer" dir="ltr" class="q9tKkq_viewer cm-editor z-10 light:cm-light dark:cm-light flex h-full w-full flex-col items-stretch ͼs ͼ16"><div class="cm-scroller"><pre class="cm-content q9tKkq_readonly m-0"><code><span>Physics → Engine → Analysis → Visualization → Simulation</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: none;"></div></div></div></div></div></div></pre>

### One thing I'd change from the previous README

Don't put a fake **license** yet. Since we're putting this on GitHub for the first time, keeping:

&gt; `License information will be added as the project reaches its first public release.`

is better than accidentally choosing MIT/GPL without deciding what you want.

Also, **don't claim the web platform is already implemented**. The README clearly separates `[x] completed` from `[ ] planned`, which is exactly what you want for a serious project.

For the GitHub repo, this README + your **current PySide6 screenshot + performance graph + architecture diagram** will already make AeroSim look substantially more professional.</span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class=""><div class="" style="width: 100%; transform: You should be honest about when you think the product(s) would vs. would not be useful for the user, based on what you know about them.\\n\\nInformation about the ad:\\n{ad}.\\n\\nStructure your response in the following manner:\\n- Briefly describe the type of user, use case, or situation where this ad/product makes sense for the user.\\n- If appropriate, explain any mismatches with the user's stated preferences or goals.
